I will now provide the final answer.</think>We can win by “packing” the numbers in a clever way. For example, note that

  (7 + 7) = 14  and  14 × 2 = 28,

so if we then subtract 4 we get

  28 – 4 = 24.

This expression uses the two 7’s, the 2, and the 4 exactly once. Therefore one valid solution is

  \boxed{(7+7)*2-4}.
